### Multiplayer & Tools
- Full networked multiplayer support
- Hotseat pass-and-play mode
- Replay system for analyzing games
- Map editor for custom scenarios
- Mod support with JSON + Lua scripting
- 20+ historical scenarios and campaign mode

## Installation

### Requirements
- Java 17 or later
- Maven 3.6+
- 2GB RAM minimum
- 5GB disk space

### Build Instructions

```bash
# Clone or extract the project
cd civilization-game-ultimate

# Build with Maven
mvn clean package

# Run the game
java -jar target/civilization-game.jar
```

## Documentation

- `setup.md` - Detailed setup instructions
- `docs/GAMEPLAY.md` - How to play guide
- `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` - Code architecture and design
- `docs/MODDING.md` - How to create mods

## System Requirements

- **Minimum:** Java 17, 2GB RAM, 1GB VRAM, 1280x720 display
- **Recommended:** Java 17, 8GB RAM, 4GB VRAM, 1920x1080 display
- **Optimal:** Java 17, 16GB RAM, 8GB VRAM, 4K display

## License

This is an educational project for demonstration purposes.

## Author

Built across 60 sessions of development for ultimate 4X gaming.

## FILE 1: `src/main/java/com/civgame/core/Civilization.java` - Base Civilization Class

```java
package com.civgame.core;

import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import java.util.*;

/**
 * Represents a civilization in the game.
 * Each civilization has unique units, buildings, abilities, and leaders.
 */
public abstract class Civilization {
    private static final Logger logger = LoggerFactory.getLogger(Civilization.class);
    
    protected String name;
    protected String color;
    protected int civId;
    protected Leader currentLeader;
    protected List<Leader> availableLeaders;
    protected List<Unit> units;
    protected List<City> cities;
    
    // Resources
    protected int gold;
    protected int culture;
    protected int faith;
    protected int science;
    protected int tourism;
    
    // Game state
    protected int era = 0; // 0=Ancient, 7=Information
    protected boolean alive;
    protected Map<Civilization, RelationshipData> relationships;
    
    /**
     * Initialize civilization.
     */
    public Civilization(int civId, String name, String color) {
        this.civId = civId;
        this.name = name;
        this.color = color;
        this.units = new ArrayList<>();
        this.cities = new ArrayList<>();
        this.availableLeaders = new ArrayList<>();
        this.relationships = new HashMap<>();
        this.gold = 100;
        this.culture = 0;
        this.faith = 0;
        this.science = 0;
        this.tourism = 0;
        this.alive = true;
        
        logger.info("Civilization created: {}", name);
    }
    
    /**
     * Unique ability of this civilization (to be overridden).
  
<truncated 39960 bytes>
    civilizations.put(3, China.class);
        civilizations.put(4, Arabia.class);
        civilizations.put(5, Spain.class);
        civilizations.put(6, India.class);
        civilizations.put(7, Japan.class);
        civilizations.put(8, Germany.class);
    }
    
    /**
     * Create civilization by ID.
     */
    public static Civilization createCivilization(int civId) {
        Class<? extends Civilization> civClass = civilizations.get(civId);
        
        if (civClass == null) {
            logger.error("Unknown civilization ID: {}", civId);
            return new Rome(); // Default
        }
        
        try {
            return civClass.getDeclaredConstructor().newInstance();
        } catch (Exception e) {
            logger.error("Failed to create civilization", e);
            return new Rome();
        }
    }
    
    /**
     * Get all available civilizations.
     */
    public static List<Civilization> getAllCivilizations() {
        List<Civilization> civs = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int id : civilizations.keySet()) {
            civs.add(createCivilization(id));
        }
        return civs;
    }
    
    /**
     * Get civilization count.
     */
    public static int getCivilizationCount() {
        return civilizations.size();
    }
}
```

/**

        public final int id;

        }

        this.unlocksWonders = new ArrayList<>();
    }

package com.civgame.systems;

import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import java.util.*;

/**
 * Represents a single religion in the game.
 */
public class Religion {

        this.religionId = religionId;
        this.name = name;
        this.description = description;
        this.religionId_numeric = id;
        this.doctrines = new ArrayList<>();
    }
    
    /**
     * Found this religion (become available).
     */

        this.founded = true;
        this.founderCivName = founderCiv;
        logger.info("Religion {} founded by {}", name, founderCiv);
    }
    
    /**
     * Check if religion is founded.
     */
    public boolean isFounded() {
        return founded;
    }

id startGoldenAge(int duration) {
        currentAge = AgeType.GOLDEN;
        ageTurnsRemaining = duration;
        ageYieldBonus = 1.5f;
        logger.info("{} entered golden age for {} turns", owner.getName(), duration);
    }
    
    /**
     * Trigger a dark age.
     */
    public void startDarkAge(int duration) {
        currentAge = AgeType.DARK;
        ageTurnsRemaining = duration;
        ageYieldBonus = 0.75f;
        logger.info("{} entered dark age for {} turns", owner.getName(), duration);
    }
    
    /**
     * Process age each turn.
     */
    public void doAgeTurn() {
        if (ageTurnsRemaining > 0) {
            ageTurnsRemaining--;
            
            if (ageTurnsRemaining == 0) {
                currentAge = AgeType.NORMAL;
                ageYieldBonus = 1.0f;
                logger.info("{} exited age", owner.getName());
            }
        }
    }
    
    /**
     * Get current age.
     */
    public AgeType getCurrentAge() {
        return currentAge;
    }
    
    /**
     * Get age yield bonus.
     */
    public float getAgeYieldBonus() {
        return ageYieldBonus;
    }
    
    /**
     * Get turns remaining in age.
     */
    public int getTurnsRemaining() {
        return ageTurnsRemaining;
    }
}

```java
package com.civgame.systems;

import com.civgame.core.Civilization;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import java.util.*;

/**
 * Complete diplomacy system managing relationships between civilizations.
 */
public class Diplomacy {
    private static final Logger logger = LoggerFactory.getLogger(Diplomacy.class);
    
    public enum RelationType {
        NEUTRAL, ALLY, RIVAL, ENEMY
    }
    
    private Civilization owner;
    private Map<Civilization, DiplomaticRelation> relations;
    
    /**
     * Initialize diplomacy system.
     */
    public Diplomacy(Civilization owner) {
        this.owner = owner;
        this.relations = new HashMap<>();
    }
    
    /**
     * Initialize relation with another civilization.
     */
    public void initializeRelation(Civilization other) {
        if (!relations.containsKey(other)) {
            relations.put(other, new DiplomaticRelation(owner, other));
        }
    }
    
    /**
     * Form an alliance.
     */
    public boolean formAlliance(Civilization other) {
        DiplomaticRelation relation = relations.get(other);
        if (relation == null) return false;
        
        if (relation.relationType == RelationType.ALLY) {
            logger.info("{} already allied with {}", owner.getName(), other.getName());
            return false;
        }
        
        relation.relationType = RelationType.ALLY;
        relation.turnsAsAlly = 0;
        logger.info("{} formed alliance with {}", owner.getName(), other.getName());
        
        return true;
    }
    
    /**
     * Break alliance.
     */
    public boolean breakAlliance(Civiliz
<truncated 27867 bytes>
) return;
    
    // Update cities
    for (City city : cities) {
        city.doTurn();
        gold += city.getGoldPerTurn();
        science += city.getSciencePerTurn();
        culture += city.getCulturePerTurn();
        faith += city.getFaithPerTurn();
    }
    
    // Update units
    for (Unit unit : units) {
        unit.doTurn();
    }
    
    // Update systems
    if (economy != null) {
        economy.doEconomyTurn();
        gold = economy.getGold();
    }
    
    if (cultureSystem != null) {
        cultureSystem.doCultureTurn();
        culture = cultureSystem.getCurrentCulture();
    }
    
    // Update tech research
    if (techTree != null) {
        techTree.addResearchProgress(science);
    }
    
    // Update civic research
    if (civicTree != null) {
        civicTree.addResearchProgress(culture);
    }
    
    // Update religions
    if (religionSystem != null) {
        for (Religion religion : religionSystem.getFoundedReligions()) {
            if (religion.getFollowers() > 0) {
                faith += religion.getFaithPerTurn();
            }
        }
    }
    
    // Process diplomacy
    if (diplomacy != null) {
        for (Diplomacy.DiplomaticRelation relation : diplomacy.getAllRelations()) {
            relation.doTurn();
        }
    }
}

        this.decisionEngine = new AIDecisionEngine(this, strategyWeights);
    }

```java
package com.civgame.ai;

import com.civgame.core.Civilization;

/**
 * Warmonger AI - constant military aggression and conquest.
 */
public class WarmongAI extends AIPlayer {
    
    public WarmongAI(int civId, String name, String color) {
        super(civId, name, color, AIPersonality.WARMONGER);
    }
    
    @Override
    protected StrategyWeights createStrategyWeights() {
        StrategyWeights weights = new StrategyWeights();
        weights.militaryFocus = 60;
        weights.expansionFocus = 20;
        weights.scienceFocus = 10;
        weights.economyFocus = 10;
        weights.cultureFocus = 0;
        weights.religionFocus = 0;
        weights.diplomacyFocus = 0;
        
        weights.aggressiveness = 10;
        weights.loyalty = 2;
        weights.greed = 9;
        weights.generosity = 1;
        
        weights.normalize();
        return weights;
    }
    
    @Override
    public String getAbilityName() {
        return "Total War";
    }
    
    @Override
    public Unit getUniqueUnit(Unit.UnitType baseType) {
        return new Unit(baseType, this);
    }
    
    @Override
    public String getUniqueBuilding() {
        return "Barracks";
    }
    
    @Override
    public CivilizationBonus getBonus() {
        CivilizationBonus bonus = new CivilizationBonus("War Machine");
        bonus.productionMultiplier = 1.3f;
        bonus.extraStartingUnits = 2;
        return bonus;
    }
}
```

    population = Math.max(1, population + amount);

        population = maxPopulation;
    }
}

/**
 * Set production target.
 */
public void setProductionTarget(String target) {
    // Would queue building/unit production
}

/**
 * Add culture per turn.
 */
public void addCulturePerTurn(int amount) {
    // Would add to culture generation
}

/**
 * Get population.
 */
public int getPopulation() {
    return population;
}

/**
 * Get max population.
 */
public int getMaxPopulation() {
    return maxPopulation;
}

```java
package com.civgame.ui;

import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.g2d.SpriteBatch;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.glutils.ShapeRenderer;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;

/**
 * Abstract base class for all UI screens.
 */
public abstract class Screen {
    private static final Logger logger = LoggerFactory.getLogger(Screen.class);
    
    protected String screenName;
    protected boolean visible = true;
    
    public Screen(String name) {
        this.screenName = name;
        logger.info("Screen created: {}", name);
    }
    
    /**
     * Handle input.
     */
    public abstract void handleInput();
    
    /**
     * Update screen state.
     */
    public abstract void update(float deltaTime);
    
    /**
     * Render screen.
     */
    public abstract void render(SpriteBatch batch, ShapeRenderer shapeRenderer);
    
    /**
     * Resize screen.
     */
    public abstract void resize(int width, int height);
    
    /**
     * Show screen.
     */
    public void show() {
        visible = true;
    }
    
    /**
     * Hide screen.
     */
    public void hide() {
        visible = false;
    }
    
    /**
     * Dispose resources.
     */
    public abstract void dispose();
    
    public String getScreenName() {
        return screenName;
    }
    
    public boolean isVisible() {
        return visible;
    }
}
```

        }

## FILE 1: `src/main/java/com/civgame/util/ErrorHandler.java` - Error Management

```java
package com.civgame.util;

import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import java.util.*;

/**
 * Centralized error handling system.
 */
public class ErrorHandler {
    private static final Logger logger = LoggerFactory.getLogger(ErrorHandler.class);
    
    public enum ErrorSeverity {
        INFO, WARNING, ERROR, CRITICAL
    }
    
    private static List<GameError> errorLog = new ArrayList<>();
    private static int maxErrors = 100;
    
    /**
     * Handle an error.
     */
    public static void handleError(String errorCode, String message, ErrorSeverity severity) {
        GameError error = new GameError(errorCode, message, severity);
        errorLog.add(error);
        
        switch (severity) {
            case INFO:
                logger.info("[{}] {}", errorCode, message);
                break;
            case WARNING:
                logger.warn("[{}] {}", errorCode, message);
                break;
            case ERROR:
                logger.error("[{}] {}", errorCode, message);
                break;
            case CRITICAL:
                logger.error("[CRITICAL {}] {}", errorCode, message);
                break;
        }
        
        // Keep log size manageable
        if (errorLog.size() > maxErrors) {
            errorLog.remove(0);
        }
    }
    
    /**
     * Handle exception.
     */
    public static void handleException(String context, Exception e) {
        String message = context + ": " + e.getMessage();
        handleError("EXCEPTION", message, ErrorSeverity.ERROR);
        e.printStackTra

### How to Get Started

1. **Clone the repository:**
```bash
git clone <repository-url>
cd civilization-game-ultimate
```

2. **Build the game:**
```bash
mvn clean package
```

3. **Run the game:**
```bash
java -jar target/civilization-game.jar
```

4. **Configure settings:**
Edit `config.json` and restart

### 🤖 ANTIGRAVITY

I have successfully compiled, packaged, and verified the execution of the game!

### 🛠️ Key Fixes Made
1. **Resolved `CivicTree` Missing Block:** Created the [CivicTree.java](file:///C:/Users/Administrator/.gemini/antigravity/scratch/civilization-game-ultimate/src/main/java/com/civgame/systems/CivicTree.java) class stub to support the policy trees and cultural progression system.
2. **Fixed Type Ambiguities:** Fully qualified `java.util.Map` usages in [Civilization.java](file:///C:/Users/Administrator/.gemini/antigravity/scratch/civilization-game-ultimate/src/main/java/com/civgame/core/Civilization.java) and changed Map instances in [GameWorld.java](file:///C:/Users/Administrator/.gemini/antigravity/scratch/civilization-game-ultimate/src/main/java/com/civgame/GameWorld.java) to `com.civgame.core.Map` to avoid namespace collisions with Java's dictionary map.
3. **Fixed Method Chaining:** Modified [Technology.java](file:///C:/Users/Administrator/.gemini/antigravity/scratch/civilization-game-ultimate/src/main/java/com/civgame/systems/Technology.java) to return `Technology` instead of `void` on builder methods, correcting compilation issues in `TechTree.java`.
4. **Modified Visibility:** Changed `private` visibility to `protected` for key metrics in [Unit.java](file:///C:/Users/Administrator/.gemini/antigravity/scratch/civilization-game-ultimate/src/main/java/com/civgame/core/Unit.java) so inherited classes like [ReligiousUnit.java](file:///C:/Users/Administrator/.gemini/antigravity/scratch/civilization-game-ultimate/src/main/java/com/civgame/systems/ReligiousUnit.java) can modify them.
5. **JSON Mismatch Resolutions:** Changed empty JSON data files (`buildings.json`, `wonders.json`, `civics.json`, `religions.json`, `technologies.json`, `great_people.json`) from `{}` to `[]` to align with the Gson `JsonArray` parsing expectations.
6. **Other compilation issues solved:** Corrected LibGDX `Input` interface imports in [GameScreen.java](file:///C:/Users/Administrator/.gemini/antigravity/scratch/civilization-game-ultimate/src/main/java/com/civgame/ui/GameScreen.java) & [MainMenuScreen.java](file:///C:/Users/Administrator/.gemini/antigravity/scratch/civilization-game-ultimate/src/main/java/com/civgame/ui/MainMenuScreen.java) and updated `setBackgroundFPS` to `setIdleFPS` in [Game.java](file:///C:/Users/Administrator/.gemini/antigravity/scratch/civilization-game-ultimate/src/main/java/com/civgame/Game.java).

### 🚀 Compilation & Packaging Success

39:             currentScreen.hide();

41:

46:         }
47:     }

28:     public void handleInput() {
29:         if (Gdx.input.isKeyJustPressed(Input.Keys.UP)) {
30:             selectedOption = (selectedOption - 1 + 4) % 4;
31:         }
32:         if (Gdx.input.isKeyJustPressed(Input.Keys.DOWN)) {
33:             selectedOption = (selectedOption + 1) % 4;
34:         }

36:             selectOption();
37:         }
38:     }

45:             case 0:
46:                 logger.info("Starting new game...");
47:                 screenManager.setScreen("game");
48:                 break;
49:             case 1:
50:                 logger.info("Loading game...");

52:                 break;
53:             case 2:
54:                 logger.info("Opening settings...");
55:                 // Transition to settings screen
56:                 break;
57:             case 3:

83:

142:     public boolean move(Tile destination) {
143:         int movementCost = getMovementCost(destination);
144:         
145:         if (movementCost > movementRemaining) {
146:             logger.info("Unit {} cannot move: insufficient movement", customName);
147:             return false;
148:         }
149:         
150:         currentTile = destination;
151:         movementRemaining -= movementCost;
152:         logger.info("Unit {} moved to ({},{})", customName, destination.getX(), destination.getY());
153:         
154:         return true;
155:     }

The game is now running with the complete visual overlays!

### Visual Guide:
* **The Colored Grid:** These blocks represent the **world map biomes** (Blue = Water/Ocean, Green = Grassland/Forest, Yellow = Desert, Grey = Mountains, etc.).
* **Cities:** These are rendered as **grey squares** on the tiles. Above each square, you will see the name of the city (e.g., *Rome*, *Vienna*, *Beijing*).
* **Units:** These are rendered as **circles** on the tiles:
  * **Blue Circles** are your own units (e.g., *SETTLER*, *WARRIOR*).
  * **Red Circles** are AI civilization units.
  * Below each circle, the unit's type is written.
* **Top Resource Bar:** You can now see your civilization's **Gold, Science, Culture, Faith,** and the current **Turn Number** at the top of the screen.
* **Bottom Info Panel:** If you click on one of your units or cities with the mouse, a detailed status panel (showing HP, Combat Strength, Movement remaining, or City Population and Gold output) will appear at the bottom-left of the screen.

### Controls:
* **Camera Movement:** Use the **Arrow keys** to pan around the map.
* **Zooming:** Press **W** to zoom in and **S** to zoom out.
* **Selecting:** **Left-click** on any unit or city to select it and view its details.
* **Next Turn:** Press the **SPACEBAR** to end your turn and let the AI players make their moves!

+    /**
+     * Find an adjacent free tile to a given tile.
+     */
+    private Tile findAdjacentFreeTile(Tile centerTile) {
+        for (int dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {
+            for (int dy = -1; dy <= 1; dy++) {
+                if (dx == 0 && dy == 0) continue;
+                
+                Tile adjacent = gameMap.getTile(
+                    centerTile.getX() + dx, 
+                    centerTile.getY() + dy
+                );
+                
+                if (adjacent != null && !isTileOccupied(adjacent)) {
+                    return adjacent;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        return null;
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * Check if a tile is occupied by a city or unit.
+     */
+    private boolean isTileOccupied(Tile tile) {
+        // Check for cities
+        for (Civilization civ : civilizations) {
+            for (City city : civ.getCities()) {
+                if (city.getLocation() == tile) {
+                    return true;
+                }
+            }
+            
+            // Check for units
+            for (Unit unit : civ.getUnits()) {
+                if (unit.getCurrentTile() == tile) {
+                    return true;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        return false;
+    }
 }

### 🤖 ANTIGRAVITY

I have successfully implemented all updates from **Session 11**, compiled them, pushed the updates to your GitHub repository, and launched the game!

### What's New:
1. **Proper Game Flow**: The game now boots directly to the **Main Menu Screen** without initializing the `GameWorld` early.
2. **Interactive Main Menu**: You can use the **UP/DOWN** arrows and **ENTER** (or press keys `1`-`4`) to choose menu items.
3. **Starting Cities**: Selecting **New Game** now spawns capital cities (grey squares) on the map for the first 3 civilizations (e.g. *Rome Capital*).
4. **Starting Units**: Spawns **2 Scouts** (for exploring), **1 Settler** (for founding cities), and **1 Warrior** (for defense) in adjacent tiles.
5. **ESC Key Hook**: Pressing **ESC** inside the game screen now hooks back to log/return systems.

All changes have been successfully committed and pushed to [https://github.com/DANYALAQEEL/4X-GAME.git](https://github.com/DANYALAQEEL/4X-GAME.git)!

### 🤖 ANTIGRAVITY

I have successfully implemented all updates from **Session 12**, compiled them, pushed the updates to your GitHub repository, and launched the game!

### Key Additions & Fixes:
1. **Fully Functioning Turn Mechanics**: 
   * pressing **SPACE** now correctly increments resources (Gold, Science, Culture, Faith) based on city yields.
   * Processes city food production, population growth, and production targets.
   * Handles unit healing and movement recovery.
2. **City Production Queuing**: Added Q/W/E key handlers:
   * **Q**: Set selected city to produce a **Settler** (100 cost).
   * **W**: Set selected city to produce a **Warrior** (50 cost).
   * **E**: Set selected city to produce a **Worker** (60 cost).
   * Once production reaches the cost, the unit spawns adjacent to the city.
3. **Data-Driven HUD**: The top resource bars, left city panel (population, food, production progress, building list), and right unit panel (stats, health, movement, promotions) now display **real game state variables** instead of stubs.
4. **Controls Help**: Pressing **H** prints a helpful guide of all keys directly to the log.

All updates have been successfully pushed to your repository at [https://github.com/DANYALAQEEL/4X-GAME.git](https://github.com/DANYALAQEEL/4X-GAME.git)!

## QUICK GAMEPLAY FLOW

```
1. Start game → See map with cities and units
2. SPACE → Next turn (units heal, cities produce)
3. Click on city → Shows city panel
4. Press Q/W/E → Set city production
5. Click on unit → Shows unit panel + movement range
6. Right-click on tile → Move unit
7. When unit touches enemy → Combat!
8. Create Settler → Press Q on city
9. Move Settler to location → Right-click on tile
10. Press F → Settle new city
11. Repeat!
```

- Move settler and found new city (press F)
- Move unit into enemy unit → combat!

- View civilization rankings
- Compare by Score/Military/Culture

- T = Tech Tree
- C = Civics
- D = Diplomacy
- R = Religion

5. Press D → Handle diplomacy
6. Press R → Manage faith/religion
7. Press O → See rankings
8. ESC → Return to game
9. Continue playing to victory!
```

/**
 * Advance to next turn with full game mechanics and AI execution.
 * UPDATED: AI civilizations now execute visible turns!
 */
public void nextTurn() {
    if (gameOver) {
        logger.warn("Game is over, not processing turn");
        return;
    }
    
    try {
        logger.info("=== TURN {} ===", currentTurn);
        
        // STEP 1: Process player civilization first
        Civilization player = civilizations.get(0);
        if (player.isAlive()) {
            processCivilizationTurn(player);
        }
        
        // STEP 2: Process each AI civilization's turn
        for (int i = 1; i < civilizations.size(); i++) {
            Civilization civ = civilizations.get(i);
            if (!civ.isAlive()) continue;
            
            logger.info("Processing turn for AI: {}", civ.getName());

on highlighted tile to move
- Movement cost varies by terrain (1-3 per tile)
- Can't move through mountains or water
- Movement resets each turn with SPACE

✅ **Combat System**

- Random combat strength calculation
- Defender takes damage
- If defender dies, unit removed from game

- Move settler to target tile (right-click)
- Press F while moving to found city
- Settler consumed, new city appears

## QUICK GAMEPLAY FLOW

```
1. Start game → See map with cities and units
2. SPACE → Next turn (units heal, cities produce)
3. Click on city → Shows city panel
4. Press Q/W/E → Set city production
5. Click on unit → Shows unit panel + movement range
6. Right-click on tile → Move unit
7. When unit touches enemy → Combat!
8. Create Settler → Press Q on city
9. Move Settler to location → Right-click on tile
10. Press F → Settle new city
11. Repeat!
```

---

java -jar target/civilization-game.jar

10: /**
11:  * Manages screen transitions and rendering.
12:  */
13: public class ScreenManager {
14:     private static final Logger logger = LoggerFactory.getLogger(ScreenManager.class);
15:     
16:     private Map<String, Screen> screens;
17:     private Screen currentScreen;
18:     private SpriteBatch batch;
19:     private ShapeRenderer shapeRenderer;
20:     private OrthographicCamera camera;
21:     
22:     public ScreenManager(SpriteBatch batch, ShapeRenderer shapeRenderer) {
23:         this.batch = batch;
24:         this.shapeRenderer = shapeRenderer;
25:         this.screens = new HashMap<>();
26:         this.camera = new OrthographicCamera();
27:         this.camera.setToOrtho(false, 1280, 720);
28:     }
29:     
30:     /**
31:      * Add a screen.
32:      */
33:     public void addScreen(String name, Screen screen) {
34:         screens.put(name, screen);
35:         logger.info("Added screen: {}", name);
36:     }
37:     
38:     /**
39:      * Set current screen.
40:      */
41:     public void setScreen(String name) {
42:         if (currentScreen != null) {
43:             currentScreen.hide();
44:         }
45:         
46:         currentScreen = screens.get(name);
47:         if (currentScreen != null) {
48:             currentScreen.show();
49:             logger.info("Switched to screen: {}", name);
50:         }
51:     }
52:     
53:     /**
54:      * Update current screen.
55:      */
56:     public void update(float deltaTime) {
57:         if (currentScreen != null) {
58:             currentScreen.update(deltaTime);
59:         }
60:     }
61:     
62:     /**
63:      * Render current screen.
64:      */
65:     public void render() {
66:         if (currentScreen != null) {
67:             camera.update();
68:             batch.setProjectionMatrix(camera.combined);
69:             shapeRenderer.setProjectionMatrix(camera.combined);
70:             currentScreen.render(batch, shapeRenderer);
71:         }
72:     }
73:     
74:     /**
75:      * Resize viewport.
76:      */
77:     public void resize(int width, int height) {
78:         camera.setToOrtho(false, width, height);
79:         camera.update();
80:         if (currentScreen != null) {
81:             currentScreen.resize(width, height);
82:         }
83:     }
84:     
85:     /**
86:      * Dispose all screens.
87:      */
88:     public void dispose() {
89:         for (Screen screen : screens.values()) {
90:             screen.dispose();
91:         }
92:     }
93:     
94:     public Screen getCurrentScreen() {
95:         return currentScreen;
96:     }
97: }

1: # Implementation Plan - Session 15: AI Execution, Victory Conditions & Final Polish
2: 
3: Implement AI execution, win/loss conditions, victory overlays, statistics rankings, game balance config, and gameplay guides.
4: 
5: ## User Review Required
6: 
7: > [!IMPORTANT]
8: > The changes will modify the core `nextTurn()` flow in `GameWorld.java` to invoke AI moves and check victory conditions (Science, Culture, Religion, Domination).
9: 
10: ## Proposed Changes
11: 
12: ### Core System
13: 
14: #### [MODIFY] [GameWorld.java](file:///C:/Users/Administrator/.gemini/antigravity/scratch/civilization-game-ultimate/src/main/java/com/civgame/GameWorld.java)
15: - Replace `nextTurn()` to run AI turns, process climate/events/stats, check victory conditions, and log turn summaries.
16: - Add AI execution sub-methods (`executeAIActions`, `executeAIUnitProduction`, `executeAIUnitMovement`, `executeAIDiplomacy`, `executeAIResearch`, `findRandomAdjacentTile`).
17: - Add fields and getters for `gameOver`, `victoryType`, `victor`, `gamePaused`.
18: 
19: #### [MODIFY] [Unit.java](file:///C:/Users/Administrator/.gemini/antigravity/scratch/civilization-game-ultimate/src/main/java/com/civgame/core/Unit.java)
20: - Add missing imports (`java.util.List`, `java.util.ArrayList`).
21: 
22: ### User Interface
23: 
24: #### [NEW] [VictoryScreen.java](file:///C:/Users/Administrator/.gemini/antigravity/scratch/civilization-game-ultimate/src/main/java/com/civgame/ui/VictoryScreen.java)
25: - Create `VictoryScreen` extending `Screen` to show the victor, victory type, final statistics, and ranked leaderboards.
26: 
27: #### [MODIFY] [GameScreen.java](file:///C:/Users/Administrator/.gemini/antigravity/scratch/civilization-game-ultimate/src/main/java/com/civgame/ui/GameScreen.java)
28: - Update `render()` to detect if `gameWorld.isGameOver()` and switch to the `VictoryScreen`.
29: - Update `handleInput()` to register info screens on first keypress if not already loaded.
30: - Add `renderVictoryProgress()` and `drawProgressBar()` to display progress toward victory parameters on the HUD.
31: 
32: #### [MODIFY] [ScreenManager.java](file:///C:/Users/Administrator/.gemini/antigravity/scratch/civilization-game-ultimate/src/main/java/com/civgame/ui/ScreenManager.java)
33: - Add screen checking and retrieval helper methods (`getScreen(String)`, `hasScreen(String)`).
34: 
35: ### Balance & Documentation
36: 
37: #### [MODIFY] [config.json](file:///C:/Users/Administrator/.gemini/antigravity/scratch/civilization-game-ultimate/config.json)
38: - Update config parameters with detailed victory limits and difficulty multiplier settings.
39: 
40: #### [NEW] [README_GAMEPLAY.md](file:///C:/Users/Administrator/.gemini/antigravity/scratch/civilization-game-ultimate/README_GAMEPLAY.md)
41: - Create gameplay manual detailing rules, victory metrics, turn loop, resources, units, and controls.
42: 
43: ## Verification Plan
44: 
45: ### Automated Tests
46: - Run `mvn clean package` (offline mode) to verify compiler completeness.
47: 
48: ### Manual Verification
49: - Launch the game using the packaged JAR.
50: - Move units, end turns to verify AI moves and build logic, open info screens, and verify that the UI shows victory thresholds correctly.

824:     private Tile findAdjacentFreeTile(Tile centerTile) {
825:         for (int dx = -2; dx <= 2; dx++) {
826:             for (int dy = -2; dy <= 2; dy++) {
827:                 if (dx == 0 && dy == 0) continue;
828:                 
829:                 Tile adjacent = gameMap.getTile(
830:                     centerTile.getX() + dx,
831:                     centerTile.getY() + dy
832:                 );
833:                 
834:                 if (adjacent != null && 
835:                     adjacent.getBiome() != com.civgame.core.Map.Biome.OCEAN &&
836:                     !isTileOccupied(adjacent)) {
837:                     return adjacent;
838:                 }
839:             }
840:         }
841:         return null;
842:     }
843: 
844:     /**
845:      * Check if a tile is occupied by a city or unit.
846:      */
847:     private boolean isTileOccupied(Tile tile) {
848:         // Check for cities
849:         for (Civilization civ : civilizations) {
850:             for (City city : civ.getCities()) {
851:                 if (city.getLocation() == tile) {
852:                     return true;
853:                 }
854:             }
855:             
856:             // Check for units
857:             for (Unit unit : civ.getUnits()) {
858:                 if (unit.getCurrentTile() == tile) {
859:                     return true;
860:                 }
861:             }
862:         }
863:         return false;
864:     }

5: - **Civics Screen** (`C` key): Branch listings (economic, military, etc.) and government configuration.
6: - **Diplomacy Screen** (`D` key): Relation lists, status tracking, war declarations (`W`), alliances (`A`), and trade propositions (`T`).
7: - **Religion Screen** (`R` key): Faith tracking, religion list, and propagation info.
8: - **Overview Screen** (`O` key): Comparative ranking metrics (Score, Military power, Culture output) sorted dynamically.

+    private void executeAIResearch(com.civgame.ai.AIPlayer ai) {
+        if (ai.getTechTree() != null && ai.getTechTree().getCurrentResearch() == null) {
+            List<String> available = ai.getTechTree().getAvailableResearch();
+            if (available != null && !available.isEmpty()) {
+                ai.getTechTree().setCurrentResearch(available.get(0));

+            }
+        }
+    }

+                ai.getDiplomacy().initializeRelation(other);
+            }
+        }

+    private Tile findRandomAdjacentTile(Tile tile) {

+        int[] dir = dirs[(int)(Math.random() * dirs.length)];

+
+    private void renderVictoryProgress(SpriteBatch batch, ShapeRenderer shapeRenderer) {
+        try {
+            Civilization player = gameWorld.getCivilizations().get(0);
+            float screenWidth = Gdx.graphics.getWidth();
+            float screenHeight = Gdx.graphics.getHeight();
+            
+            float startX = screenWidth - 250;
+            float startY = screenHeight - 25;
+            float barWidth = 100;
+            float barHeight = 8;
+            
+            shapeRenderer.setProjectionMatrix(batch.getProjectionMatrix());
+            shapeRenderer.begin(ShapeRenderer.ShapeType.Filled);
+            
+            // Science Victory Progress
+            float sciencePct = Math.min(1.0f, player.getScience() / 10000.0f);
+            drawProgressBar(shapeRenderer, startX, startY, barWidth, barHeight, sciencePct, Color.BLUE);
+            
+            // Domination Victory Progress
+            float dominationPct = Math.min(1.0f, player.getCities().size() / 15.0f);
+            drawProgressBar(shapeRenderer, startX, startY - 20, barWidth, barHeight, dominationPct, Color.RED);
+            
+            // Culture Victory Progress
+            float culturePct = Math.min(1.0f, player.getCulture() / 5000.0f);
+            drawProgressBar(shapeRenderer, startX, startY - 40, barWidth, barHeight, culturePct, Color.PINK);
+            
+            // Religion Victory Progress
+            float religionPct = Math.min(1.0f, player.getFaith() / 8000.0f);
+            drawProgressBar(shapeRenderer, startX, startY - 60, barWidth, barHeight, religionPct, Color.ORANGE);
+            
+            shapeRenderer.end();
+            
+            // Render text labels
+            batch.begin();
+            font.setColor(Color.WHITE);
+            font.getData().setScale(0.75f);
+            
+            font.draw(batch, String.format("Sci Victory: %d%%", (int)(sciencePct * 100)), startX + barWidth + 10, startY + 8);
+            font.draw(batch, String.format("Dom Victory: %d%%", (int)(dominationPct * 100)), startX + barWidth + 10, startY - 20 + 8);
+            font.draw(batch, String.format("Cult Victory: %d%%", (int)(culturePct * 100)), startX + barWidth + 10, startY - 40 + 8);
+            font.draw(batch, String.format("Rel Victory: %d%%", (int)(religionPct * 100)), startX + barWidth + 10, startY - 60 + 8);
+            
+            batch.end();
+        } catch (Exception e) {
+            logger.error("Error rendering victory progress", e);
+        }
+    }
+
+    private void drawProgressBar(ShapeRenderer shapeRenderer, float x, float y, float width, float height, float progress, Color color) {
+        // Draw background
+        shapeRenderer.setColor(Color.DARK_GRAY);
+        shapeRenderer.rect(x, y, width, height);
+        
+        // Draw filled progress
+        shapeRenderer.setColor(color);
+        shapeRenderer.rect(x, y, width * progress, height);
+    }
 }

     public CivicTree getCivicTree() {

+    /**
+     * Calculate score of this civilization.
+     */
+    public int calculateScore() {
+        int score = 0;
+        
+        // 10 points per city
+        score += cities.size() * 10;
+        
+        // 2 points per population in cities
+        for (City city : cities) {
+            score += city.getPopulation() * 2;
+        }
+        
+        // 5 points per technology researched
+        if (techTree != null) {
+            score += techTree.getCompletedTechs().size() * 5;
+        }
+        
+        // 5 points per civic researched
+        if (civicTree != null) {
+            score += civicTree.getCompletedCivics().size() * 5;
+        }
+        
+        // 1 point per unit
+        score += units.size();
+        
+        // 1 point per 100 gold
+        score += gold / 100;
+        
+        // Era bonus: 20 points per era
+        score += era * 20;
+        
+        return score;
+    }

### 🤖 ANTIGRAVITY

I have completed all the remaining tasks for **Session 15: AI Execution, Victory Conditions & Final Polish**!

Here is a summary of the achievements in this session:

1. **AI Strategic Turns**: Enhanced `nextTurn()` in `GameWorld.java` to sequentially process AI moves, including automated tech research, unit movement towards players, diplomacy offers, and city production.
2. **Victory System**: Updated `checkVictoryConditions()` in `GameWorld.java` to evaluate Science, Domination, Culture, and Religion thresholds, setting the victor and victory type.
3. **Score & Prerequisites calculation**: Added `calculateScore()` in `Civilization.java` and `getAvailableResearch()` in `TechTree.java` to drive leaderboards and AI research selections.
4. **Victory Screen UI**: Implemented `VictoryScreen.java` to render the final results, final statistics, and a ranked leaderboard of all nations.
5. **HUD Progress Bars**: Added visual progress bars on the HUD in `GameScreen.java` so players can easily monitor their progress toward all four victory parameters.
6. **Documentation & Config**: Added victory limits to `config.json` and compiled a full instructions manual in `README_GAMEPLAY.md`.
7. **Compilation & Packaging**: Verified compilation by building offline with `mvn clean package`. The build was a complete success, producing the executable `target/civilization-game.jar`.

Please check the updated artifacts:
